On behalf of the Board of Star Plus Legend Holdings Limited, I am         本人代表巨星傳奇集團有限公司董事會，榮幸地
honored to present the annual report of the Group for FY2025.             提呈本集團 2025 財年的年度報告。

In 2025, the global economic environment remained challenging,            2025 年，全球經濟環境依然充滿挑戰，而中國 IP
and the IP and consumption market in the PRC witnessed a clear            及消費市場呈現明顯分化態勢。面對品牌客戶營
divergence. Facing external pressures such as tightening marketing        銷預算收緊、短視頻內容對長視頻注意力分流等

from long-form to short-form video, we have resolutely advanced our       務結構，將資源聚焦於高潛力的 IP 消費賽道。儘
strategic transformation, proactively optimized our business structure,   管外部環境複雜，我們在調整業務重心的過程中
and focused our resources on the high-potential IP consumption            穩步前進，積極應對市場變化帶來的挑戰。
segments. Despite the complex external environment, we have made
steady progress in adjusting our business priorities and actively
addressed the challenges posed by market changes.

where we attract traffic through celebrity IPs and popular contents       熱 門 內 容 吸 引 流 量 進 而 賦 能 我 們 產 品 的 銷 售。

BUSINESS OUTLOOK                                                               業務展望
Looking ahead, while fortifying its existing core businesses, the Group        展望未來，本集團將在鞏固現有核心業務的基礎
will continue to focus its efforts on three strategic directions: firstly,     上，聚焦三大戰略方向持續發力：一是拓展多元
expanding a diversified IP matrix and deepening the development                化的 IP 矩陣，深化自有及合作 IP 的開發與運營；
and operation of its proprietary and collaborative IPs; secondly,              二是豐富產品組合，圍繞明星 IP 與大健康賽道推
enriching the product portfolio by launching more innovative products          出更多具市場競爭力的創新產品；三是強化全渠
with market competitiveness centered on celebrity IP and the big               道佈局，加速發展線上線下與境內境外融合的銷
health sector; and thirdly, strengthening the omni-channel layout to           售網絡，以提升 IP 與產品的觸達效率與變現能力。
accelerate the development of an integrated online and offline as
well as domestic and overseas sales network, thereby enhancing the
reach efficiency and monetization potential of both IPs and products.

In terms of IP matrix expansion, the Group completed several                   在 IP 矩陣拓展方面，集團於 2025 年完成了若干具
strategically significant equity investments in 2025, aiming at building       有戰略意義的股權投資，旨在為未來 IP 生態構建
a more robust resource base for its future IP ecosystem. In particular,        更豐富的資源基礎。其中，集團成為國家體育場（鳥
the Group has become one of the shareholders of the operating entity           巢）運 營 主 體 的 股 東 之 一，未 來 將 積 極 探 索 在 大

NON-COMPETITION UNDERTAKING                                                 不競爭承諾
On 29 May 2023, each of the Controlling Shareholders has entered            於 2023 年 5 月 29 日，各 控 股 股 東 已 以 本 公 司（為
into a deed of non-competition (the “Deed of Non-competition”) in           其本身及作為其附屬公司的受託人）為受益人訂
favour of the Company (for itself and as trustee for its subsidiaries)      立不競爭契據（「不競爭契據」），據此，各控股股
under which each of the Controlling Shareholders has, warranted and         東已向本公司作出保證及承諾，其將不會，且將
undertaken to our Company that he/she/it shall not, and he/she/it           盡最大努力促使其緊密聯繫人（本集團任何成員

so to encourage them to work towards enhancing the value of the             價值。合資格參與購股權計劃的人士為 (i) 本集團
Company. Persons eligible to participate the Share Option Scheme            任何成員公司的任何董事及僱員；(ii) 本集團任何

subsidiaries or associated companies of the Group; and (iii) any            般及正常業務過程中持續或經常性地向本集團提
person (including an entity) that provides services to the Group on         供符合其長期增長利益的服務（「服務提供商」）。

The Board has adopted a board diversity policy which sets out              董事會已採納董事會多元化政策，當中載列實現
the approach to achieve diversity on the Board. The Company                董事會多元化的方法。本公司深知設有多元化董
recognizes the importance and benefits of having a diverse Board           事會的重要性及裨益，並視董事會層面日益多元
and sees increasing diversity at Board level as an essential element       化為實現本公司戰略目標及可持續發展的要素。
in achieving the Company’s strategic goals and sustainable                 本公司務求通過考慮眾多因素以實現董事會多元
development. The Company seeks to achieve Board diversity through          化，該等因素包括但不限於專業知識、技能、經驗、
the consideration of a number of factors, including but not limited to     獨 立 性、年 齡、文 化、種 族 及 性 別。本 公 司 將 考
expertise, skills, experience, independence, age, culture, ethnicity       慮董事會多元化政策及其他因素，同時基於對董
and gender. The Company will select potential Board candidates             事會的潛在貢獻挑選潛在董事會候選人。董事會
based on his or her potential contribution to the Board while taking       的所有委任均以用人唯才為原則，並在充分顧及
into account the board diversity policy and other factors. All Board       董事會多元化的裨益後以客觀標準考慮候選人。
appointments will be based on meritocracy and candidates will
be considered against objective criteria, having due regard to the
benefits of diversity on the Board.

INVESTMENT POLICY AND OBJECTIVES                                            投資政策及目標
The Company has adopted an internal investment policy (the                  本公司已採納內部投資政策（「投資政策」
                                                                                              ），其中
“Investment Policy”) which sets out, among other things, the                載列（其中包括）本集團所進行投資活動的目標、
objectives, guidelines, management and responsibilities of investment       指引、管理及責任。下列所載為本集團投資基礎
activities conducted by the Group. Set out below are details of the         設施的詳情：
infrastructure of the Group’s investments:

Investment objectives                                                       投資目標
The investment objectives of the Group are to enhance the efficiency        本集團的投資目標是提高閒置資金的運用效率，
in the utilization of idle funds and generate stable return to the Group    在可接受的風險水平下為本集團帶來穩定的回報，
within an acceptable risk level with a view to preserve and grow its        以期保值及增值其資產，從而為其股東提升價值。
assets which in turn enhance value for its Shareholders.

The purpose of these investments is to generate potential value,            該等投資的目的是創造潛在價值，為營運需求維
maintain sufficient liquidity for operational needs, and foster potential   持充足的流動性，並促進與其他企業的潛在協同
synergies with other enterprises to support future growth.                  效應以支持未來增長。

Investment strategy                                                         投資策略
The Company will manage the aggregate portfolios to ensure the              本公司將透過股份、債券、投資基金、保險產品
safety and preservation of capital through shares, bonds, investment        及銀行存款或類似金融產品管理整體投資組合，
funds, insurance products and bank deposits, or similar financial           以確保資本安全及保值，同時使投資組合的到期
products, while aligning portfolio maturities and durations with            日及存續期與預期流動性需求及營運資金需求相
anticipated liquidity requirements and working capital needs.               匹配。

The Company will allocate corporate resources efficiently by                本公司將透過維持適當的投資規模及優化其投資
maintaining an appropriate investment scale and optimizing the              組合的架構及多元化來有效分配公司資源。
structure and diversification of its investment portfolio.

Investment scope                                                            投資範圍
The Company’s investment activities encompass both long-term and            本公司的投資活動涵蓋長期及短期投資，取決於
short-term investments, depending on its strategic needs and the            其策略需求及當前市場狀況。長期投資著重增長
prevailing market conditions. Long-term investments focus on growth         及策略，而短期投資則注重流動性及穩定回報。
and strategy, while short-term investments prioritize liquidity and
stable return.

RISK MANAGEMENT AND CONTROL MEASURES                                        風險管理及控制措施
The intent of the investment policy is to manage the portfolio to           投資政策旨在管理投資組合，以將風險降至最低

 The Group applied the HKFRS 9 simplified approach to measure ECL               •      For trade and other receivables assessed individually, evaluated
 which used a lifetime expected loss allowance for trade receivables and               the appropriateness of management estimates and judgments by
 involved complex management estimates and judgements. For trade                       assessing the historical payment profiles, subsequent settlements
 receivables with objective evidence of impairment, management assessed                and day past due information;
 ECL individually. The expected loss rates were determined based on the         •      對於單獨評估的貿易及其他應收款項，通過評估歷史付款記錄、
 historical payment profiles, the subsequent settlements and day past due              隨後結算及逾期天數資料，評估管理層估計及判斷的適當性；
 information. For trade receivables without objective evidence of impairment,
 management estimated ECL by grouping trade receivables based on their
 nature and risk characteristics and then analysing their aging information
 before further incorporating forward-looking adjustments to reflect
 management’s forecasts of macroeconomic factors in different scenarios.
 貴集團應用香港財務報告準則第 9 號簡化法計量預期信貸虧損，該方
 法對貿易應收款項採用全期預期虧損撥備，並涉及複雜的管理層估計
 及判斷。對於有減值客觀證據的貿易應收款項，管理層單獨評估預期
 信貸虧損。預期虧損率乃根據歷史付款記錄、隨後結算及逾期天數資
 料釐定。對於並無減值客觀證據的貿易應收款項，管理層通過根據其
 性質及風險特徵將貿易應收款項分組，然後分析其賬齡資料，再進一
 步納入前瞻性調整以反映管理層在不同情況下對宏觀經濟因素的預測，
 從而估計預期信貸虧損。

      principally engaged in new retail business and IP creation              和 國（「中 國」）從 事 新 消 費 業 務 以 及 IP 創 造

3     FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                           3     財務風險管理
3.1 Financial risk factors                                                3.1 財務風險因素
      The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks:         本集團業務面臨多種財務風險：市場風險（包
      market risk (including foreign exchange risk, cash flow and               括外匯風險、現金流量及公平值利率風險以
      fair value interest rate risk and price risk), credit risk and            及價格風險）、信貸風險及流動資金風險。
      liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk management programme             本集團的整體風險管理計劃專注於金融市
      focuses on the unpredictability of financial markets and seeks            場的不可預測性，並尋求盡量降低對本集團
      to minimise potential adverse effects on the Group’s financial            財務表現可能構成的不利影響。
      performance.

      Financial risk management is carried out by the finance                   財務風險管理在董事會監督下由財務部執行。
      department under the supervision of the Board of Directors.               董事會制訂整體風險管理的原則。
      The board provides principles for overall risk management.

(a)   Market risk                                                         (a)   市場風險
(i)   Foreign exchange risk                                               (i)   外匯風險
      Foreign exchange risk arises from future commercial                       外匯風險產生自以實體功能貨幣以外的貨
      transactions or recognised assets and liabilities denominated             幣計值的未來商業交易或已確認資產及負債。
      in a currency that is not the functional currency of the relevant
      Group entity.

      The Group manages its foreign exchange risk by closely                    本集團透過密切監察外匯匯率的變動管理
      monitoring the movement of the foreign currency rates.                    其外匯風險。從中國匯出的現金須遵守中國
      Cash repatriation from the PRC are subject to the rules and               政府頒佈的外匯管制規則及條例。本集團並
      regulations of foreign exchange control promulgated by the                無其他重大外匯風險。
      PRC government. The Group did not have other significant
      exposure to foreign exchange risk.

      The carrying amount of the Group’s foreign currency                       本集團以外幣計值的貨幣資產及貨幣負債
      denominated monetary assets and monetary liabilities at the               於綜合財務狀況表相關日期的賬面值如下：
      respective dates of consolidated statements of financial position
      are as follows:

(a)     Market risk (Continued)                                          (a)     市場風險（續）
(i)     Foreign exchange risk (Continued)                                (i)     外匯風險   （續）
        As at 31 December 2025, the Group’s entities with functional             於 2025 年 12 月 31 日，本 集 團 功 能 貨 幣 為
        currency of HK$ had aggregate US$ net monetary assets of                 港元的實體合共美元貨幣資產淨值為人民
        RMB169,812,000 (2024: RMB777,000). Under the linked                      幣 169,812,000 元（2024 年：人民幣 777,000
        exchange rate system in Hong Kong, HK$ is pegged to US$,                 元）。在香港的聯絡匯率制度下，港元與美
        management therefore considers that there is no significant              元掛鈎，因此管理層認為港元兌美元並無重
        foreign exchange risk with respect to US$.                               大外匯風險。

(ii)    Cash flow and fair value interest rate risk                      (ii)    現金流量及公平值利率風險
        As the Group has no significant interest-bearing assets except           由於本集團並無重大計息資產（現金及現金
        for cash and cash equivalents and restricted bank deposits,              等價物以及受限制銀行存款除外），故本集
        the Group’s income and operating cash flows are substantially            團的收入及經營現金流量基本上不受市場
        independent of changes in market interest rates.                         利率變動的影響。

        The Group’s interest-rate risk mainly arises from borrowings.            本集團的利率風險主要來自借款。按浮動利
        Borrowings obtained at variable rates expose the Group to                率取得的借款使本集團面對現金流量利率
        cash flow interest-rate risk. The Group’s borrowings as at 31            風險。本集團於 2025 年 12 月 31 日的借款均
        December 2025 were all at fixed interest rate and their fair             按固定利率計息，且由於短期到期，其公平
        value approximated their carrying value due to short-term                值與其賬面值相若。因此，利率風險並不重大。
        maturity. Thus the interest rate risk is insignificant.

(iii)   Price risk                                                       (iii)   價格風險
        The Group is exposed to equity price risk mainly arising from            本集團面臨股本價格風險，主要產生於本集
        investments held by the Group that are classified as FVPL. To            團持有並分類為按公平值計入損益的金融
        manage its price risk arising from the investments, the Group            資產的投資。為管理投資產生的價格風險，
        diversifies its investment portfolio. The investments are made           本集團將投資組合多樣化。投資乃為戰略目
        either for strategic purposes, or for the purpose of achieving           的或為同時實現投資收益及平衡本集團流
        investment yield and balancing the Group’s liquidity level               動性水平而作出。每項投資均由管理層按具
        simultaneously. Each investment is managed by management                 體情況管理。由管理層進行的敏感度分析載
        on a case by case basis. The sensitivity analysis performed by           於附註 3.3(iii)。
        management is set out in Note 3.3(iii).

      Credit risk is managed on a group basis. The credit risk of                 信貸風險按組合基準管理。本集團的信貸風
      the Group mainly arises from financial assets, cash and cash                險主要來自金融資產、現金及現金等價物、
      equivalents, restricted bank deposits and trade and other                   受限制銀行存款以及貿易及其他應收款項。
      receivables. The carrying amounts of these balances represent               該等結餘的賬面值指本集團就該等資產所
      the Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk in relation to                  面臨的最大信貸風險。
      these assets.

      In order to minimise the credit risk, management has delegated              為盡量降低信貸風險，管理層已委派一支負
      a team responsible for determination of credit limits, credit               責確定信貸限額、信貸審批及其他監督程序
      approvals and other monitoring procedures to ensure that                    的團隊，以確保採取跟進措施收回逾期債務。
      follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts. Before                  於接納新債務人前，本集團會評估潛在債務
      accepting any new debtor, the Group assesses the potential                  人的信貸質素並界定債務人的信貸限額。本
      debtor’s credit quality and defines credit limits by debtor. Credit         集團定期審閱授予債務人的信貸限額。此外，
      limits granted to debtors are reviewed regularly. In addition,              本集團於報告期末審閱各個別債項的可收
      the Group reviews the recoverable amount of each individual                 回金額，確保已就不可收回金額計提足夠的
      debt at the end of the reporting period to ensure that adequate             減值虧損。就此而言，管理層認為本集團的
      impairment losses are made for irrecoverable amounts. In this               信貸風險大大降低。
      regard, management considers that the Group’s credit risk is
      significantly reduced.

      The Group’s bank balances are deposited in banks and                        本集團的銀行結餘均存放於中國及香港信
      financial institutions with good reputation in the PRC and Hong             譽良好的銀行及金融機構。因此，該等銀行
      Kong. Accordingly, the credit risk on these bank balances is                結餘的信貸風險有限。

3   FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT (Continued)                                3   財務風險管理（續）
3.2 Capital risk management                                              3.2 資本風險管理
    The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard            本集團管理資金的目標旨在保障本集團按
    the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order              持續經營基準繼續營運的能力，以為股東帶
    to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other               來回報，同時兼顧其他利益相關方的利益，
    stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to             並維持最佳的資本架構以減低資本成本。
    reduce the cost of capital.

    In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group          為維持或調整資本架構，本集團可能調整向
    may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders,                 股東支付的股息金額、向股東退回資本、發
    return capital to shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets          行新股份或出售資產，從而減少債務。
    to reduce debt.

    The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio.            本集團根據資本負債比率監控資本。該比率
    This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital. Net       按債務淨額除以總資本計算。債務淨額按借
    debt is calculated as total borrowings and lease liabilities less        款總額及租賃負債減現金及現金等價物以
    cash and cash equivalents and restricted bank deposits. Total            及受限制銀行存款計算。總資本按綜合財務
    capital is calculated as “equity” as shown in the consolidated           狀況表所示的「權益」加債務淨額計算。
    statement of financial position plus net debt.

      This section explains the judgments and estimates made in                   本節闡述釐定於財務報表中確認及按公平
      determining the fair values of the financial instruments that               值計量的金融工具公平值所作出的判斷及
      are recognised and measured at fair value in the financial                  估計。為得出有關釐定公平值所用輸入數據
      statements. To provide an indication about the reliability of               的可靠性指標，本集團將其金融工具分為三
      the inputs used in determining fair value, the Group has                    個層級：
      categorised its financial instruments into three levels as follows:

      Level 1:    The fair value of financial instruments traded in               第一層： 在活躍市場上買賣的金融工具（如
                  active markets (such as publicly traded derivatives                  公開買賣的衍生工具及股本證券）
                  and equity securities) is based on quoted market                     的公平值乃按於報告期末的市場
                  prices at the end of the reporting period. The                       報價釐定。本集團所持有金融資
                  quoted market price used for financial assets                        產採用的市場報價為當時買盤價。
                  held by the Group is the current bid price. These                    該等工具計入第一層。
                  instruments are included in level 1.

      Level 2:    The fair value of financial instruments that are not            第二層： 未在活躍市場買賣的金融工具（例
                  traded in an active market (for example, over-the-                   如場外衍生工具）的公平值利用
                  counter derivatives) is determined using valuation                   估值技術釐定，該等估值技術盡
                  techniques which maximise the use of observable                      量利用可觀察市場數據，並盡量
                  market data and rely as little as possible on entity-                減少依賴實體特定估計。倘計量
                  specific estimates. If all significant inputs required               工具公平值所需所有重大輸入數
                  to fair value an instrument are observable, the                      據均可觀察，則該工具計入第二
                  instrument is included in level 2.                                   層。

      Level 3:    If one or more of the significant inputs is not based           第三層： 倘一項或多項重大輸入數據並非
                  on observable market data, the instrument is                         根據可觀察市場數據得出，則該
                  included in level 3. This is the case for investment                 工具計入第三層。對於並無可觀
                  in certain WMPs without observable market data.                      察市場數據的若干理財產品投資
                                                                                       而言亦是如此。

      JUDGMENTS
      Estimates and judgments are continually evaluated and are               估計及判斷會持續進行評估，並根據過往經
      based on historical experience and other factors, including             驗及其他因素而作出，包括對未來事件作出
      expectations of future events that are believed to be reasonable        認為在有關情況下屬合理的預期。
      under the circumstances.

      The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the                本集團對未來作出估計及假設。如其定義，
      future. The resulting accounting estimates will, by definition,         所得的會計估計將很少與相關實際結果一
      seldom equal the related actual results. The estimates and              致。存在重大風險可能導致須對下個財政年
      assumptions that have a significant risk of causing a material          度的資產及負債賬面值作出重大調整的估
      adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities            計及假設討論如下。
      within the next financial year are discussed below.

      indicator such as whether the Group controls specified services           務轉移至客戶前是否控制另一方提供的特

(b)   Impairment of trade and other receivables                           (b)   貿易及其他應收款項減值
      The Group management determine the provision for                          本集團的管理層根據對貿易及其他應收款

      assessment of the expected credit losses of these receivables.            的減值撥備。評估乃基於過往虧損經驗作出，
      The assessment is based on the historical loss experience,                並進行調整以反映當前市場狀況及前瞻性

      provision at each reporting date. Details of the expected credit
      loss assessment are set out in Note 3.1(b).

(c)   Estimation of the fair value of certain financial assets            (c)   若干金融資產公平值估計
      The fair value of financial instruments that are not traded in an         並無於活躍市場交易的金融工具的公平值
      active market is determined using valuation techniques. The               採用估值技術釐定。本集團運用其判斷選擇
      Group uses its judgement to select a variety of methods and               多種方法，並主要根據各報告期末存在的市
      make assumptions that are mainly based on market conditions               況作出假設。所用主要假設及輸入數據的詳
      existing at the end of each reporting period. Details of the key          情披露於附註 3.3。
      assumptions and inputs used are disclosed in Note 3.3.

      of manufacturing and selling products of similar nature. It               偏好的變化及競爭對手為應對嚴峻的行業

(d)   Accounting policies of revenue recognition                         (d)   收入確認的會計政策
      Revenue is recognised when or as the control of goods or                 收入於貨品或服務的控制權轉移至客戶時
      services is transferred to the customer. Depending on the terms          確認。視乎合約條款及適用於該合約的法律
      of the contract and the laws that apply to the contract, revenue         規定，收入可隨時間或於某一時間點確認。
      may be recognised over time or at a point in time.

      Control of the goods or services is transferred over time if the         倘本集團符合下列其中一項情況，貨品或服
      Group:                                                                   務的控制權將隨時間轉移：

      –     provides all of the benefits received and consumed                 －   提供所有獲客戶同時收取並耗用的利
            simultaneously by the customer;                                        益；

      –     creates or enhances an asset that the customer controls            －   因本集團履約而產生或增加客戶所控
            as the Group performs; or                                              制的資產；或

      –     does not create an asset with an alternative use to                －   並無產生對本集團而言有其他用途的
            the Group and the Group has an enforceable right to                    資 產，而 本 集 團 可 強 制 執 行 其 權 利 收
            payment for performance completed to date.                             回至今已完成履約的款項。

      If control of the goods and services transfers over time, revenue            倘貨品及服務的控制權隨時間轉移，則收入
      is recognised on a straight-line basis over the terms agreed                 按合約協定的期限或電視節目或播放期間

      Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the                 及服務控制權的某一時間點確認。
      customer obtains control of the goods and services.

      is measured based on time-based measure of progress                          約責任表現的以時間為基礎的進度計量方

      performance obligation.

      standalone selling price. The Group generally determines                     據向客戶收取的價格釐定獨立售價。倘獨立
      standalone selling prices based on the prices charged to                     售價無法直接觀察得出，則會視乎可觀察資
      customers. If the standalone selling price is not directly                   料的可用性，使用預計成本加利潤或經調整
      observable, it is estimated using expected cost plus a margin                市場評估法進行估算。在估算各項不同履約
      or adjusted market assessment approach, depending on                         責任的相對售價時已作出假設及估計，倘更
      the availability of observable information. Assumptions and                  改對該等假設及估計的判斷，則可能會影響
      estimations have been made in estimating the relative selling                收入確認。
      price of each distinct performance obligation, and changes in
      judgments on these assumptions and estimates may impact
      the revenue recognition.

      If a customer pays consideration or the Group has a right to                 在本集團向客戶轉讓貨品或服務前，倘若客

       The following is a description of the accounting policy for the             以下是本集團主要收入來源的會計政策描述：
       principal revenue streams of the Group:

(i)    Revenue from new retail business                                     (i)    新消費業務收入
       (1)     Sales of health management, skincare and other products             (1)   銷售健康管理、護膚品及其他產品
               Revenue from the sale of goods is recognised at point                     銷售貨品所得收入於資產控制權轉移
               in time when control of the asset is transferred to the                   至 客 戶 時 確 認，於 產 品 驗 收 或 交 付 產
               customer, which is upon the acceptance of the products                    品 時 確 認。本 集 團 確 認 的 收 入 相 當 於
               or delivery of goods. The Group recognises revenue in                     合 約 銷 售 價 格 減 增 值 稅、銷 售 退 貨 的
               an amount equal to the contract sales prices less value-                  估 計 津 貼、批 次 折 扣 及 支 付 予 分 銷 商
               added tax, estimated allowances for sales returns, volume                 的 獎 勵。銷 售 退 貨 的 津 貼、批 次 折 扣
               discounts and incentives paid to distributors. Allowances                 及給予分銷商的獎勵是根據合約條款
               for sales returns, volume discounts and incentives to                     及歷史模式估計得出。
               distributors are estimated based on contract terms and
               historical patterns.

               The Group is regarded as the principal in the sales                       本 集 團 被 視 為 銷 售 交 易 的 主 理 人，因
               transactions as (a) the Group is the primary obligator                    為 (a) 本集團是向分銷商提供指定貨品
               of providing the specified goods or services to its                       或服務的主要責任人；(b) 本集團承擔
               distributors; (b) the Group retains the inventory risk and                所有存貨風險並負責產品的交付；(c)
               is responsible for the delivery of products; (c) the Group                本集團可全權酌情釐定健康管理及護
               has the sole discretion in determining the pricing policy                 膚 品 的 定 價 政 策，以 及 促 銷 所 需 的 折
               for the health management and skincare products and                       扣、獎 勵 及 費 用。因 此，銷 售 健 康 管
               the discounts, incentives and fees necessary for the                      理及護膚品所得收入按毛額確認。

              financial statements and after offsetting accumulated                 的 10% 作 為 法 定 儲 備 金。當 法 定 盈 餘
              losses from prior years) to the statutory reserve fund. The           儲備累計結餘等於或超過註冊資本的

              equal to or more than 50% of the registered capital.                  有關標準，並已停止撥付。

36 RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                        36 關聯方交易
   Parties are considered to be related if one party has the           倘一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或在
   ability, directly or indirectly, to control the other party or      作出財務及經營決策方面能對另一方行使
   exercise significant influence over the other party in making       重大影響力，則雙方被視為關聯方。倘雙方
   financial and operating decisions. Parties are also considered      受共同控制、共同重大影響或聯合控制，亦
   to be related if they are subject to common control, common         被視為關聯方。
   significant influence or joint control.

   As mentioned in note 1, the Company is jointly controlled           誠 如 附 註 1 所 述，本 公 司 由 控 股 股 東（即 馬
   by the controlling shareholders, being Ms. Ma, Hsin-Ting,           心婷女士、楊峻榮先生、葉惠美女士及陳中
   Mr. Yang, Chun-Jung, Ms. Yeh Hui-Mei and Mr. Chen,                  先生）共同控制，彼等根據一致行動人士協
   Chung, who are acting in concert pursuant to a concert party        議一致行動。各控股股東、主要管理成員及
   agreement. Each of the controlling shareholders, members of         其近親家庭成員（包括由彼等控制或可行使
   key management and their close family members, including            重大影響力的公司）均被視為本集團的關聯
   companies controlled by them or they can exercise significant       方。
   influence, are considered as related parties of the Group.

   In the opinion of the directors, the related party transactions     董事認為，關聯方交易乃於正常業務過程中
   were carried out in normal course of business and at terms          按本集團與各關聯方磋商之條款進行。
   negotiated between the Group and the respective related
   parties.

40.1 Subsidiaries                                                           40.1 附屬公司
     Subsidiaries are all entities over which the Group has control.           附屬公司指本集團擁有控制權的所有實體。
     The Group controls an entity where the Group is exposed to,               倘本集團因參與實體營運而承擔風險或有
     or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with the          權享有當中的可變動回報，且能夠透過其管
     entity and has the ability to affect those returns through its            理該實體業務的權力影響該等回報，則本集
     power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are fully      團控制該實體。附屬公司自控制權轉移至本
     consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to             集團之日起全面綜合入賬，自控制權終止日
     the Group. They are deconsolidated from the date that control             期起終止綜合入賬。
     ceases.

     The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for               本集團使用會計收購法為並非共同控制下
     business combinations not under common control by the                     的業務合併入賬。
     Group.

     Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains                 公司間交易、結餘及集團公司間交易的未變
     on transactions between group companies are eliminated.                   現收益均予對銷。除非交易提供證據顯示所
     Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the transaction              轉讓資產已減值，否則未變現虧損亦會對銷。
     provides evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.              附屬公司的會計政策已於必要時作出更改，
     Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed where               確保與本集團所採納的政策一致。
     necessary to ensure consistency with the policies adopted by
     the Group.

     Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of                    附屬公司業績及權益內的非控股權益於綜
     subsidiaries are shown separately in the consolidated financial           合財務報表中單獨列示。
     statements.

      consolidated financial statements are presented in RMB which

      such transactions and from the translation at period-end                     外匯盈虧於綜合全面（虧損）╱收益表「其他

40.6 Property, plant and equipment                                      40.6 物業、廠房及設備
     Property, plant and equipment are stated at historical cost less     物業、廠房及設備乃按歷史成本減累計折舊

     attributable to the acquisition of the items.

     Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s carrying amount         僅當有關項目的未來經濟利益可能流入本
     or recognised as a separate asset, as appropriate, only when         集團，且該項目的成本能夠可靠地計量時，

     the item will flow to the Group and the cost of the item can be      立 資 產（如 適 用）。置 換 部 分 的 賬 面 值 將 被
     measured reliably. The carrying amount of the replaced part is       終止確認。所有其他維修保養費用於產生的
     derecognised. All other repairs and maintenance are charged          財務期間在綜合全面（虧損）╱收益表內扣除。
     to the consolidated statement of comprehensive (loss)/income
     during the financial period in which they are incurred.

     using the straight-line method to allocate their cost to their       於其估計可使用年期內將其成本攤分至其

     Furniture, fixture and    5–10 years                                 傢具、固定裝置           5 至 10 年
       equipment                                                           及設備
     Leasehold                 Over the shorter of their expected         租賃裝修              預期可使用年期及租
       improvements              useful lives and the lease terms                            賃期之較短者

     The assets’ residual values and useful lives are reviewed, and       資產的剩餘價值及可使用年期在各財務狀
     adjusted if appropriate, at each financial position date.            況日期進行檢討，並視乎情況作出調整。

     than its estimated recoverable amount (Note 40.9).                   註 40.9）。

40.8 Investment properties                                                 40.8 投資物業
      Investment properties, commercial plazas, are held for long-               投資物業、商業廣場乃為長期租金收益而持
      term rental yields. Investment property is initially measured              有。投資物業初始按成本計量，包括相關交
      at cost, including related transaction costs. Depreciation on              易成本。投資物業折舊採用直線法計算，以
      investment property is calculated using the straight-line method           於 10 年內將成本分配至剩餘價值。
      to allocate the cost to residual values over 10 years.

40.9 Impairment of non-financial assets                                    40.9 非金融資產減值
      Assets that are subject to amortisation are tested for                     當發生事件或情況轉變，顯示賬面值可能無
      impairment whenever events or changes in circumstances                     法收回時，則對須攤銷的資產進行減值測試。
      indicate that the carrying amount may not be recoverable. An               減值虧損按資產賬面值超出其可收回金額
      impairment loss is recognised for the amount by which the                  的金額確認。可收回金額為資產公平值減銷
      asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable amount. The                售成本與使用價值兩者的較高者。於評估減
      recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less             值時，資產按獨立可識別現金流入的最低層
      costs to sell and value in use. For the purposes of assessing              次分組，有關現金流入大致上獨立於其他資
      impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest levels for which              產或資產組別（現金產生單位）的現金流入。
      there are separately identifiable cash inflows which are largely           在各報告期末，將審閱出現減值的非金融資
      independent of the cash inflows from other assets or groups of             產（商譽除外）是否有撥回減值的可能。
      assets (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets other than
      goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed for possible
      reversal of the impairment at the end of each reporting period.

40.11 Offsetting financial instruments                                    40.11 抵銷金融工具
     Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount           當有法定可強制執行權利可抵銷已確認金
     reported in the consolidated statement of financial position             額，並有意按淨額基準結算或同時變現資產
     when there is a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised       及償付負債時，金融資產與負債可互相抵銷，

     realise the asset and settle the liability simultaneously. The           制執行權利不得依賴未來事件而定，而在一

     business and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy           制執行。
     of the Group or the counterparty.

40.17 Trade and other payables                                               40.17 貿易及其他應付款項
     Trade payables are obligations to pay for goods or services that           貿易應付款項為於日常業務過程中向供應
     have been acquired in the ordinary course of business from                 商購入貨品或服務的付款責任。倘貿易及其
     suppliers. Trade and other payables are classified as current              他應付款項於一年或以內（或如屬較長時間，
     liabilities if payment is due within one year or less (or in the           則以業務正常經營週期為準）到期，則分類
     normal operating cycle of the business if longer). If not, they            為流動負債，否則按非流動負債呈列。
     are presented as non-current liabilities.

     Trade and other payables are recognised initially at fair value            貿易及其他應付款項初步按公平值確認，其
     and subsequently measured at amortised cost using the                      後使用實際利率法按攤銷成本計量。
     effective interest method.

40.18 Borrowings and borrowing costs                                         40.18 借款及借款成本
     Borrowings are initially recognised at fair value, net of                  借款初步按公平值確認，經扣除已產生的交
     transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently                    易成本。借款其後以攤銷成本列賬。所得款
     measured at amortised cost. Any difference between the                     項（經 扣 除 交 易 成 本）與 贖 回 金 額 之 間 的 任
     proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the redemption amount              何差額，使用實際利率法於借款期內在損益
     is recognised in profit or loss over the period of the borrowings          中確認。於建立貸款融資時支付的費用於將
     using the effective interest method. Fees paid on the                      很有可能提取部分或全部融資時確認為貸
     establishment of loan facilities are recognised as transaction             款的交易成本。於此情況下，有關費用遞延
     costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some or           至融資被提取時。倘無證據顯示將很有可能
     all of the facility will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is           提取部分或全部融資，有關費用將被資本化
     deferred until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no             為流動資金服務的預付款項並於相關融資
     evidence that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be      期間進行攤銷。
     drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a prepayment for liquidity
     services and amortised over the period of the facility to which it
     relates.

     Borrowings are removed from the balance sheet when the                     當合約中規定的責任獲解除、取消或屆滿時，
     obligation specified in the contract is discharged, cancelled              借款自資產負債表中剔除。金融負債賬面值
     or expired. The difference between the carrying amount of a               （已消除或轉撥至另一方）與已付代價（包括
     financial liability that has been extinguished or transferred to           已轉讓的任何非現金資產或所承擔負債）之
     another party and the consideration paid, including any non-               間的差額於損益中確認為融資成本。
     cash assets transferred or liabilities assumed, is recognised in
     profit or loss as finance costs.

     Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the Group          除非本集團有無條件權利將負債的結算遞
     has an unconditional right to defer settlement of the liability for        延 至 報 告 期 後 至 少 12 個 月，否 則 借 款 分 類
     at least 12 months after the reporting period.                             為流動負債。

40.19 Current and deferred income tax                                      40.19 即期及遞延所得稅
      The income tax expense for the year ended 31 December                       截至 2025 年 12 月 31 日止年度的所得稅開支
      2025 comprises current and deferred tax. Tax is recognised in               包括即期稅項及遞延稅項。稅項在綜合全面
      the consolidated statements of comprehensive (loss)/income,                （虧損）╱收益表中確認，惟與於其他全面收
      except to the extent that it relates to items recognised in other           益或直接於權益中確認的項目相關者除外。
      comprehensive income or directly in equity. In this case,                   於此情況下，稅項亦分別於其他全面收益或
      the tax is also recognised in other comprehensive income or                 直接於權益中確認。
      directly in equity, respectively.

(a)   Current income tax                                                   (a)   即期所得稅
      The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the            即期所得稅開支根據本集團營運所在及產

      statement of financial position date in the country where the              期已頒佈或實質已頒佈的稅法計算。管理層
      Group operates and generates taxable income. Management                    就適用稅務法例有待詮釋的情況定期評估
      periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect         報稅表的狀況，並於適用情況下根據預期須
      to situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to             向稅務機構支付的稅款設定撥備。
      interpretation. It establishes provisions where appropriate on
      the basis of amounts expected to be paid to the tax authorities.

(b)   Deferred income tax                                                   (b)   遞延所得稅
      Income tax is recognised, using the liability method, on                    所得稅就資產及負債的稅基與其於綜合財
      temporary differences arising between the tax bases of assets               務報表所示賬面值之間的暫時性差額以負

      not recognised if they arise from initial recognition of goodwill,          於非業務合併的交易中初步確認資產或負
      the deferred income tax is not accounted for if it is from initial          債而產生遞延所得稅，而交易當時並無影響
      recognition of an asset or liability in a transaction other than a          會計或應課稅溢利或虧損，則不會將遞延所
      business combination that at the time of the transaction affects            得稅入賬。遞延所得稅按綜合財務狀況表日
      neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred income              期前已實施或實際上已實施，並在變現有關
      tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been                 遞延所得稅資產或清償遞延所得稅負債時
      enacted or substantially enacted by the consolidated statement              預期將會適用的稅率（及法律）釐定。
      of financial position date and are expected to apply when the
      related deferred income tax asset is realised or the deferred
      income tax liability is settled.

      Deferred tax assets are recognised only if it is probable                   僅當可能有未來應課稅金額用於動用該等
      that future taxable amounts will be available to utilise those              暫時性差額及虧損時，方會確認遞延稅項資
      temporary differences and losses.                                           產。

      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised for                  在公司有能力控制暫時性差額的撥回時及
      temporary differences between the carrying amount and tax                   差額可能不會在可見未來撥回的情況下，不
      bases of investments in foreign operations where the company                會就外國業務投資的賬面值與稅基之間的
      is able to control the timing of the reversal of the temporary              暫時性差額確認遞延稅項資產及負債。
      differences and it is probable that the differences will not
      reverse in the foreseeable future.

      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset where there is               倘存在可依法強制執行權利以抵銷即期稅
      a legally enforceable right to offset current tax assets and                項資產及負債，且倘遞延稅項結餘與同一稅
      liabilities and where the deferred tax balances relate to the               務機關相關，則會抵銷遞延稅項資產及負債。
      same taxation authority. Current tax assets and tax liabilities are         倘實體擁有可依法強制執行權利進行抵銷

      and intends either to settle on a net basis, or to realise the              債，則會抵銷即期稅項資產及稅項負債。

40.21 Provisions                                                           40.21 撥備
      Provisions are recognised when: the Group has a present                    倘本集團因過往事件而承擔現有法定或推
      legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is         定責任；有可能須流出資源以履行責任；且
      probable that an outflow of resources will be required to settle           有關金額已可靠估計時，則會確認撥備。概
      the obligation; and the amount has been reliably estimated.                不會就未來經營虧損確認撥備。
      Provisions are not recognised for future operating losses.

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood            倘出現多項類似責任，履行責任時須流出資
      that an outflow will be required in settlement is determined by            源的可能性乃經考慮整體責任類別後釐定。
      considering the class of obligations as a whole. A provision is            即使就同一責任類別所包含的任何一個項
      recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to            目流出資源的可能性極低，仍須確認撥備。
      any one item included in the same class of obligations may be
      small.

      Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s               撥備按管理層對預期履行有關責任所需開
      best estimate of the expenditures expected to be required to               支的最佳估計現值採用稅前利率計量，該利
      settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate that reflects current           率反映當時市場對金錢時間值和有關責任
      market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                特定風險的評估。因時間推移而增加的撥備
      specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision due to           確認為財務成本開支。
      passage of time is recognised as finance cost expense.

            excluding any costs of servicing equity other than                        外的任何權益服務成本），除以
            ordinary shares; and

      •     by the weighted average number of ordinary shares                    •    財政年度內發行在外普通股加權平均
            outstanding during the financial year, adjusted for bonus                 數，並就年內已發行普通股（不包括庫
            elements in ordinary shares issued during the year and                    存股）的股利成分調整。
            excluding treasury shares.

      The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate              租賃付款乃使用租賃中的內含利率（倘該利

      is allocated between the liability and finance cost. The finance       財務成本。財務成本於租期內自損益扣除，

      produce a constant periodic rate of interest on the remaining          利率。
      balance of the liability for each period.

1.   SUSTAINABILITY GOVERNANCE                                        1.   可持續發展管治
1.1. Board Statement                                                  1.1. 董事會聲明
     The Board recognises that environmental, social and                   本集團董事會深知環境、社會及管治（「ESG」）
     governance (“ESG”) matters are essential to responding to             對於回應持份者日益提升的期望，以及支持
     the rising expectations of stakeholders and supporting the            本集團長期價值與業務表現至關重要。因此，
     Group’s long-term value and business performance. As the              董事會作為本集團 ESG 相關事務的最高決策
     Group’s highest decision-making and accountable body for              及責任機構，全面負責 ESG 策略的制定、信
     ESG matters, the Board is fully responsible for formulating           息披露及監督管理。董事會對本集團 ESG 策
     ESG strategies, overseeing disclosures and supervising ESG            略及匯報工作負有整體責任，並負責評估及
     management. The Board also has overall responsibility for             釐定 ESG 相關風險，確保設有適當且有效的
     the Group’s ESG strategy and reporting, and is responsible            ESG 風險管理及內部監控系統，以支援 ESG
     for assessing and determining ESG-related risks and ensuring          管理工作的有序推進。
     that appropriate and effective ESG risk management and
     internal control systems are in place to support the effective
     implementation of ESG management.

1.2. ESG Risk Management                                              1.2. ESG 風險管理
     We attach great importance to ESG-related matters in                  本集團高度重視業務經營與風險管理中的
     business operations and risk management. To effectively               ESG 相關事宜。為有效防範潛在營運風險，
     prevent potential operational risks, we have established              我們已建構完善的風險管控機制，協助董事
     a comprehensive risk control mechanism to support the                 會對各類風險（包括 ESG 相關重點風險）進行
     Board in identifying, assessing and managing various risks,           全面識別、評估及管理，以支持本集團全面
     including key ESG-related risks, thereby supporting the               及可持續營運。董事會對建立及維持適當且
     Group’s comprehensive and sustainable operations. The Board           有效的風險管理及內部監控系統負最終責任；
     has ultimate responsibility for establishing and maintaining          在本公司審核委員會的支持下，董事會每年

     systems annually. During the Reporting Period, we formulated          關風險對營運造成不必要影響的可能性。
     and implemented corresponding preventive and mitigation
     measures for identified risks to reduce the likelihood of ESG-

      We attach great importance to sustainable development and             本集團高度重視可持續發展，致力將 ESG 原

      effectively and in an orderly manner, we have established             行董事－業務部門」的管治架構，以統籌及

            internal control systems are in place to support the

1.5. Materiality Assessment                                             1.5. 重要性評估
1.5.1.Methodology                                                       1.5.1.重要性評估方法
      In accordance with the Environmental, Social and Governance             本集團遵循香港聯合交易所「ESG 報告守則」
      Reporting Guide of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited,             規定，持續加深對主要持份者就 ESG 議題之
      we continued to deepen our understanding of the concerns                關注與期望的理解。於本年度，我們以「ESG
      and expectations of key stakeholders regarding ESG issues.              報告守則」中披露層面為基礎，結合本集團
      During the year, we further refined our ESG issue library based         業務特性及行業發展趨勢，並參考可持續發
      on the disclosure aspects set out in the ESG Reporting Guide,           展 會 計 準 則 委 員 會 (SASB) 等 國 際 主 流 披 露
      taking into account our business characteristics and industry           框架及主流 ESG 評級的關注議題，持續完善
      development trends, and with reference to internationally               本集團 ESG 議題庫。其後，我們委託獨立顧
      recognised disclosure frameworks such as Sustainability                 問透過問卷調查方式與不同持份者溝通，以
      Accounting Standards Board (SASB) and the focus areas of                瞭解彼等對本集團 ESG 表現的評價及對各項
      mainstream ESG ratings. We then engaged an independent                  ESG 議題重要性的意見，作為釐定本次重要
      consultant to conduct questionnaire surveys with different              性議題的主要參考依據。同時，我們亦根據
      stakeholder groups to understand their assessment of the                持份者的關注重點及反饋結果，持續檢視並
      Group’s ESG performance and views on the importance of                  有序優化本集團的 ESG 策略與管理政策。
      individual ESG issues. These findings served as the primary
      basis for determining the material issues for this report.
      We also continued to review and refine our ESG strategy
      and management policies in an orderly manner based on
      stakeholder priorities and feedback.

      During the Reporting Period, we followed a structured “Identify       報告期內，我們遵循「識別－排序－確認」的
      – Prioritise – Confirm” process to systematically review and          科學流程，系統性回顧並優化 ESG 重要性議
      refine our ESG materiality framework.                                 題框架。

      •     Issue identification: With reference to relevant                •    議題識別：參考 SASB 相關披露指引，
            Sustainability Accounting Standards Board (SASB)                     並 結 合 市 場 趨 勢、行 業 基 準 及 重 要 性
            disclosure guidance and in light of market trends,                   參考資料，梳理並甄選出 16 項與本集
            industry benchmarks and materiality reference materials,             團業務相關的 ESG 議題。
            we identified and screened 16 ESG issues relevant to the
            Group’s business.

      •     Issue prioritisation: Internal and external stakeholders        •    議 題 排 序：邀 請 內 外 部 持 份 者 參 與 評
            were invited to assess each issue based on its impact                估，分 別 就 議 題 對 本 集 團 業 務 的 影 響
            on our business operations and its importance to                     及 對 持 份 者 的 重 要 性 作 出 評 分，並 按
            stakeholders, with results ranked by priority.                       分析結果進行優先次序排列。

      •     Issue confirmation: The materiality assessment results          •    議 題 確 認：重 要 性 評 估 結 果 經 高 級 管
            were reviewed and confirmed by senior management and                 理層及董事會審核後確認。
            the Board.

2.    ENVIRONMENT                                                        2.   環境
      We are firmly committed to green development, embedding                  本集團堅定踐行綠色發展理念，將企業環保
      environmental responsibility into our daily operations. We               責任深度融入日常營運之中。我們嚴格遵守
      strictly comply with the Environmental Protection Law of the            《中華人民共和國環境保護法》、《中華人民
      People’s Republic of China, the Law of the People’s Republic of          共和國固體廢物污染環境防治法》、《中華人
      China on the Prevention and Control of Environmental Pollution           民共和國大氣污染防治法》、《中華人民共和
      by Solid Wastes, the Law of the People’s Republic of China on            國 節 約 能 源 法》及《中 華 人 民 共 和 國 水 污 染
      the Prevention and Control of Atmospheric Pollution, the Energy          防治法》等各營運地適用的環保規例。鑒於
      Conservation Law of the People’s Republic of China, and the              本集團業務並不涉及製造及生產活動，而大
      Water Pollution Prevention and Control Law of the People’s               部分資源由供應商及第三方物流服務供應
      Republic of China, along with other applicable environmental             商提供，我們的營運對環境的直接影響相對
      regulations in our operating regions. As our business does               有限。儘管如此，本集團仍積極推進節能及
      not involve manufacturing or production activities, with most            減排措施，盡量降低商業活動對自然生態環
      resources provided by suppliers and third-party logistics                境的潛在影響。於報告期間，本集團並不知
      service providers, our operations have relatively limited direct         悉任何與中國環境法相關的重大違規情況，
      environmental impact. Nevertheless, we actively promote                  亦並不知悉任何嚴重違反或不遵守與廢氣
      energy conservation and emissions reduction measures to                  及溫室氣體排放、向水及土地的排污，以及
      minimise the potential impact of our business activities on the          有害及無害廢棄物產生相關、並對本集團造
      natural environment. During the Reporting Period, we were not            成重大影響的適用法律法規之情況。
      aware of any material non-compliance with environmental laws
      in China, nor any serious violations or non-compliance with
      applicable laws and regulations relating to air and greenhouse
      gas emissions, discharges into water and land, or generation
      of hazardous and non-hazardous waste that would have a
      significant impact on us.

      to further enhance our data collection and management                   步完善數據收集及管理系統。現階段我們尚
      systems. At present, we have not established specific                   未就減排訂立具體量化目標，惟已制定初步
      quantitative emissions reduction targets; however, we have              方向性目標及減排計劃，以逐步減少本集團
      formulated preliminary directional goals and reduction plans            排放及對環境的影響，並持續跟進目標制定
      to progressively reduce our emissions and environmental                 與落實進度。未來，我們將綜合考慮行業表
      impact, while continuously monitoring progress on target-               現及本集團過去、現時及預期的環境表現等

      industry benchmarks and our past, current, and projected                排放、廢棄物、能源及水資源等範疇制定相

      that all business activities comply with statutory ambient air          務活動符合法定的大氣環境質量標準與排放
      quality standards and emission regulations, implementing                規定，並落實相關管理措施以減少大氣污染
      management measures to reduce air pollutant emissions and               物排放及其對環境的影響。基於本集團的業
      their environmental impact. Given our business characteristics,         務特性，我們的生產環節及核心產品運輸主
      production and core product transportation are primarily                要委託第三方生產商及物流服務供應商負責，
      outsourced to third-party manufacturers and logistics service           並無直接營運任何貨運車輛，因此日常營運

      from daily operations. Relevant emissions primarily originate           汽油所產生的尾氣，以及員工宿舍爐灶燃燒
      from exhaust gases produced by diesel and petrol combustion             天然氣的排放。
      in vehicles used for business travel, as well as emissions
      from natural gas combustion in cooking stoves at employee
      dormitories.

      Although air pollutant emissions from our daily operations           儘管本集團日常營運所產生的空氣污染物
      are relatively low, we actively implement emission reduction         排放相對較少，我們仍積極推行減排管理，
      management and have adopted the following measures to                並已實施以下措施以減少空氣污染物排放：
      reduce air pollutant emissions:

            to reduce unnecessary mileage;                                       以減少不必要的行駛里程；

      •     Promote green driving habits, such as turning off engines      •     積 極 推 行 綠 色 駕 駛 習 慣，車 輛 停 泊 時
            when vehicles are stationary; and                                    關閉發動機；及

      •     Conduct regular professional maintenance and servicing         •     對車輛進行定期的專業維修與保養，
            of vehicles to reduce air pollutant emissions.                       降低空氣污染物排放。

      collaboration with Original Design Manufacturing (“ODM”)
      partners and packaging suppliers.

      with upstream and downstream value chain partners to reduce              及使用過程中對環境造成的影響。相關措施

            weight and volume, product safety and stability, cost, and             全 性 與 穩 定 性、成 本 及 市 場 趨 勢 等 因
            market trends to optimise packaging design and reduce                  素，優化包裝設計以減少不必要包裝。
            unnecessary packaging.

            environmentally friendly packaging materials and/or                    及╱或可生物降解包裝材料。

            packaging materials and actively encourage suppliers to                商，並 主 動 鼓 勵 供 應 商 採 用 更 可 持 續
            adopt more sustainable packaging components.                           的包裝組件。

     change have become increasingly frequent, posing severe              頻發，為全球生態與商業社會帶來了嚴峻考
     challenges to global ecosystems and the business community.          驗。本 集 團 積 極 響 應《巴 黎 協 定》的 氣 候 變
     We actively respond to the climate change initiatives of the         化倡議及中華人民共和國「碳達峰、碳中和」
     Paris Agreement and the People’s Republic of China’s dual-           的戰略目標。我們深明妥善應對氣候變化並
     carbon strategic goals. We understand that properly addressing       提升氣候韌性，既是合規營運的重要一環，
     climate change and enhancing climate resilience is both an           亦是支持本集團長遠可持續發展的關鍵。
     important component of compliant operations and a key factor
     in supporting our long-term sustainable development.

     During the year, we actively referred to the Implementation          於本年度，我們積極參照《香港交易所環境、
     Guidance for Climate-related Disclosures under the HKEX              社會及管治框架下氣候信息披露的實施指
     ESG Framework, disclosing key climate-related work across            引》，從 管 治、策 略、風 險 管 理、指 標 和 目
     core areas including governance, strategy, risk management,          標等核心領域披露氣候相關的重點工作。未

2.4.3.Risk Management                                                         2.4.3.風險管理
      To systematically manage climate-related matters, we are                      為系統化管控氣候相關事宜，本集團致力將
      committed to integrating climate risks into our overall enterprise            氣候風險納入整體的企業風險管理及內部
      risk management and internal control framework. Taking into                   控制框架中。結合本集團業務性質、營運地
      account our business nature, geographical footprint, and                      理佈局及內外部發展環境，並參考外部專業
      internal and external operating environment, and drawing on                   意見，我們已初步建立氣候變化相關影響、
      external professional advice, we have established preliminary                 風險及機遇的管理流程，涵蓋識別、分析、
      management processes for climate-related impacts, risks, and                  評估及應對環節。
      opportunities, covering identification, analysis, assessment, and
      response.

      •        Identification: we conduct preliminary mapping of                     •     識 別：我 們 就 氣 候 相 關 風 險 及 機 遇 進
               climate-related risks and opportunities.                                    行初步梳理；

      •        Analysis and assessment: we examine the likelihood and                •     分 析 及 評 估：我 們 按 既 定 流 程 對 其 可
               potential impact according to established processes, and                    能 性 及 潛 在 影 響 作 出 檢 視，並 持 續 進
               conduct ongoing monitoring and follow-up;                                   行監察及跟進；

      •        Response: we progressively develop and refine                         •     應 對：我 們 將 因 應 風 險 情 況 及 管 理 需
               corresponding measures in accordance with risk                              要，逐 步 制 定 及 完 善 相 應 措 施，以 提
               conditions and management needs, to enhance the                             升本集團的氣候風險管理成效。
               effectiveness of climate risk management across the
               Group.

      employment contracts with long-term employees in accordance              期僱員訂立規範的僱傭合約，以保障雙方權
      with the Labour Contract Law of the People’s Republic of China           益並維持合規用工。
      to protect the rights of both parties and maintain compliant

3.1.2.Equal Opportunity and Anti-discrimination                          3.1.2.平等機會及反歧視
      We are an equal opportunity employer committed to fostering              本集團為提供平等機會的僱主，致力營造多
      a diverse, inclusive and discrimination-free workplace in                元、反歧視及包容的工作環境，確保僱員獲
      which all employees are treated with dignity and respect.                得尊嚴及尊重對待。平等機會原則適用於招
      The principle of equal opportunity applies throughout our                聘及其他僱傭常規與政策，我們按僱員的優
      recruitment process and all other employment practices and               點作出聘用決定，並致力提供公平的就業機
      policies. We make employment decisions based on merit and                會及公平報酬。我們承諾在招聘過程中不設
      are committed to providing fair employment opportunities                 定性別、地域等差別化條件，亦不得因種族、
      and equitable remuneration. We do not impose differentiated              性別、膚色、宗教信仰、年齡、民族或殘疾
      requirements based on gender, region or other similar factors            而歧視僱員。
      during recruitment, nor do we tolerate discrimination against
      employees on the basis of race, gender, skin colour, religious
      belief, age, ethnicity or disability.

      In addition, the Group’s Whistleblowing Policy covers prohibited       同 時，本 集 團《舉 報 政 策》涵 蓋 禁 止 歧 視 或
      misconduct such as discrimination and harassment, and                  騷擾等不當行為，並提供舉報渠道及指引；
      provides reporting channels and guidelines. Whistleblowers             就任何真實及真誠的舉報，舉報人將受到保
      who make genuine and good-faith reports are protected                  護，不會因按政策作出舉報而遭解僱、受害
      and will not face dismissal, victimisation, or any form of             或招致任何形式的報復。為此，我們鼓勵遭
      retaliation for reporting in accordance with the policy. We            受任何形式歧視的僱員透過暢通渠道尋求
      therefore encourage employees who experience any form                  即時協助，以便管理層按需要作出及時調查
      of discrimination to seek immediate assistance through our             及跟進處理。
      established channels, enabling management to conduct timely
      investigations and follow-up actions where necessary.

3.1.3.Employee Benefits and Care                                          3.1.3.僱員福利及關懷
      Compensation, Benefits and Promotion                                   薪酬福利及晉升
      We are committed to attracting and retaining talent by                 本集團致力透過提供公平合理的薪酬待遇
      offering fair and reasonable compensation and benefits while           及福利，吸引及保留人才，並維持薪酬政策
      maintaining market-competitive remuneration policies. To this          的市場競爭力。為此，我們會每年審閱並按
      end, we review and adjust annual employee compensation as              需要調整僱員年度薪酬，薪酬調整將綜合考
      needed each year. Salary adjustments take into account factors         慮公司效益及僱員工作表現、職級調整情況
      including company performance, individual work performance,            以及市場薪酬趨勢等因素，以確保薪酬安排
      position changes, and market compensation trends to ensure             具備合理性及競爭力。同時，本集團每年為

3.4. Labour Standards                                                 3.4. 勞工準則
     We strictly comply with applicable laws, regulations and              本集團嚴格遵守經營所在國家及地區有關招
     practices relating to recruitment and employment management           聘及僱傭管理的適用法律法規及慣例，並對
     in the countries and regions where we operate, and adopt              任何形式的僱用童工及強制勞工行為採取「零
     a zero-tolerance approach to any form of child labour or              容忍」態度。就防範童工而言，我們依循《禁
     forced labour. To prevent child labour, we conduct rigorous           止使用童工規定》於招聘環節進行嚴格審查，
     checks during recruitment in accordance with the Provisions           由人力資源部核對求職者身份證明文件及
     on the Prohibition of Child Labour. The Human Resources               相關資料，確保其符合法定就業年齡，避免
     Department verifies applicants’ identity documents and related        僱用童工。就杜絕強制勞工而言，我們保障
     information to ensure they meet the statutory working age             僱員自由選擇就業的權利，所有僱傭關係均
     requirements and to prevent the employment of child labour.           屬自願建立；如需終止僱傭關係，亦會依法
     To eliminate forced labour, we safeguard employees’ right to          提供適當通知期或代通知金，並按既定安排
     freely choose employment, and all employment relationships            處理任何不合規情況的跟進及補救。此外，
     are established on a voluntary basis. Where termination of            本集團已制定明確的休息及休假政策，並鼓
     employment is necessary, we provide an appropriate notice             勵僱員於八小時的標準工作日內完成工作；
     period or payment in lieu of notice in accordance with the            如因工作需要安排加班，我們會在確保僱員
     law, and follow established procedures to address and rectify         健康的前提下依法作出安排，並按既定流程
     any non-compliance. In addition, we have established clear            進行管理。
     policies on rest periods and leave, and encourage employees
     to complete their work within a standard eight-hour working
     day. Where overtime is required for business needs, we make
     arrangements in accordance with the law while safeguarding
     employee health, and manage the process in line with
     established procedures.

      We prioritise product safety and consumer rights, and have                 本集團重視產品安全及消費者權益，並已建
      established a product recall management mechanism to                       立相應產品召回管理機制以保障產品質量
      safeguard product quality and maintain compliant operations.               及維持合規營運。我們嚴格遵守《中華人民
      We strictly comply with the product recall requirements under              共 和 國 食 品 安 全 法》及《中 華 人 民 共 和 國 食
      the Food Safety Law of the People’s Republic of China and                  品安全法實施條例》所載之產品召回相關規
      the Regulations on the Implementation of the Food Safety Law               定，並參照有關要求制定及落實內部召回安
      of the People’s Republic of China, and have formulated and                 排。倘我們或第三方生產商發現產品不符合
      implemented internal recall arrangements with reference to                 食品安全標準，或有證據顯示可能危害人體
      these requirements. If the Group or third-party manufacturers              健康，我們將按既定程序及時採取相應措施，
      discover that a product does not meet food safety standards,               包括立即停止相關生產及╱或經營活動、對
      or if evidence suggests that it may endanger human health,                 已上市銷售產品啟動召回、通知相關生產者、

      established procedures, including immediately ceasing the                  出記錄；如本集團認為有需要召回，亦會即
      relevant production and/or business activities, initiating recalls         時召回已上市銷售的產品。
      of products already placed on the market, notifying relevant
      producers, operators and consumers, and keeping records of

      Republic of China, the Anti-Money Laundering Law of the                  規的相關規定，對一切形式的賄賂及貪腐採
      People’s Republic of China, and the Interim Provisions on                取零容忍態度，並在業務交易中恪守商業誠
      Banning Commercial Bribery. We adopt a zero-tolerance                    信、誠實、公平、公正及透明的高標準。於
      approach to all forms of bribery and corruption, and uphold              報告期間，本集團未發現嚴重違反有關防止
      high standards of business integrity, honesty, fairness,                 賄賂、勒索、欺詐和洗錢的法律法規而對本
      impartiality and transparency in our business transactions.              集團產生重大影響，概無針對本集團或其僱
      During the Reporting Period, no serious violations of laws and           員提起有關腐敗行為、勒索及洗錢的法律案
      regulations relating to the prevention of bribery, extortion, fraud      件。
      and money laundering that had a significant impact on us were
      identified, and no legal cases relating to corrupt practices,
      extortion or money laundering were brought against us or our
      employees.

      To implement the relevant requirements, we have established              為落實相關要求，本集團已制定並推行《反
      and implemented the Anti-Corruption Policy and the                       貪腐政策》及《舉報政策》，就員工行為操守
      Whistleblowing Policy, providing clear guidelines on employee            及合規要求、與業務往來方的誠信規範、利
      conduct and compliance requirements, integrity standards                 益衝突管理、舉報及調查程序，以及培訓與
      in dealings with business partners, conflict of interest                 溝通等方面建立清晰指引。該等政策適用於
      management, reporting and investigation procedures, as well              本集團董事、高級職員及僱員，並就收受及
      as training and communication. These policies apply to our               提供利益、酬酢安排及避免利益衝突等作出
      directors, senior officers, and employees, and set out standards         規範。
      regarding the acceptance and offering of benefits, hospitality

      faith, and are committed to providing confidentiality and                舉報人提供保密及反報復保障。舉報可透過
